function	script	F_tt	{
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"main")) end;
	set .@w$,(charat(strnpcinfo(4),6))+((getstrlen(strnpcinfo(4)) > 9)?(substr(strnpcinfo(4),8,9)):(charat(strnpcinfo(4),getstrlen(strnpcinfo(4))-1)));
	return .@w$;
}

-	script	entrance#tt_main	-1,{
	end;

OnEnable:
	enablenpc();
	enablewaitingroomevent strnpcinfo(0);
	end;

OnStartArena:
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"n1")) {
		set .@in$, "n";
		set .@in, 1;
	} else {
		set .@in$,(compare(strnpcinfo(1),"Expert"))?"e":"n";
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"4")) set .@in, 4;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"8")) set .@in, 8;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"16")) set .@in, 16;
	}
	warpwaitingpc "turbo_"+.@in$+"_"+.@in,298,161;
	donpcevent "Broadcast#"+.@in$+.@in+"::OnEnable";
	disablewaitingroomevent strnpcinfo(0);
	end;
	
OnInit:
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(0),"main")) end;
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"n1")) {
		waitingroom "Solo Mode",60,"Solo Mode#n1::OnStartArena",1,1000,10;
	} else {
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"4")) set .@in, 4;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"8")) set .@in, 8;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"16")) set .@in, 16;
		waitingroom strnpcinfo(1),60,strnpcinfo(0)+"::OnStartArena",.@in,1000,10;
	}
	enablewaitingroomevent strnpcinfo(0);
	end;
}

	end;

OnTouch_:
	if (tt_point < 28999) {
		set tt_point,tt_point+2;
		warp strnpcinfo(4),59,364;
	}
	else
		warp strnpcinfo(4),59,364;
	end;

	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "Point Give-Away Guy#"+.@w$;
	end;
}

-	script	Broadcast#tt_main	-1,{
OnEnable:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "Broadcast#"+.@w$;

	end;

OnTimer2000:

OnTimer7000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"您將在 30 秒後被傳送至起跑線。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer10000:

	end;

OnTimer15000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"30 秒倒數即將開始。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer17000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩餘 30 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer27000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩餘 20 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer37000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩餘 10 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer42000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩餘 5 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer43000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩餘 4 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer44000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩餘 3 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer45000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩餘 2 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer46000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩餘 1 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer47000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"您即將被傳送至起跑線。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer49000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "Point Give-Away Guy#"+.@w$;
	donpcevent "Master#"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
	end;

OnTimer50000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	donpcevent "snake#"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
	donpcevent "hunting#"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
	if (strnpcinfo(4) != "turbo_n_1") enablenpc "bing#"+.@w$;
	end;

OnTimer57000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "Point Give-Away Guy#"+.@w$;
	end;

OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "Broadcast#"+.@w$;
	end;
}

	if (strnpcinfo(4) == "turbo_n_1") {
		donpcevent "Solo Mode#n1::OnEnable";
	} else {
		donpcevent RName(.@w$)+"::OnEnable";
	}
	enablenpc "No_Unfair_Start#"+.@w$+"-1";
	enablenpc "No_Unfair_Start#"+.@w$+"-2";

	disablenpc "Flasher_Exit_2#"+.@w$;
	end;
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "Master#"+.@w$;
	end;
	
	function	RName	{
		.@string$ = getarg(0);
		.@s = ( getstrlen(.@string$) > 2 ? substr(.@string$,1,2) : charat(.@string$,getstrlen(.@string$)-1) );
		set .@rn$,((compare(strnpcinfo(4),"_e_"+.@s))?"Expert mode":"Normal mode")+" - "+.@s+" person";
		return .@rn$;
	}
}

-	script	cos_end#tt_main	01,{
	function	GetNumber;
OnTouch_:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	if (.@w$ == "n1") set $@end_time,gettimetick(0);
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),strcharinfo(0) +" 剛抵達終點線！恭喜！",bc_map,"0xFFFF00";
	if (.@w$ != "n1") {
		setd "$ttnames$["+GetNumber(.@w$)+"]",strcharinfo(0);
		donpcevent "Turbo Track Guide::OnWin_"+.@w$;
	}
	warp strnpcinfo(4),384,161;
	if (.@w$ == "e4" || .@w$ == "n4" || .@w$ == "n1") {
		if (.@w$ == "e4") {
			donpcevent "Winner Helper#TBT_"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
		} else {
			donpcevent "Guide#TBT_"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
		}
		donpcevent "Master#"+.@w$+"::OnDisable";
		disablenpc "Broadcast#"+.@w$;
	} else {
		if (.@w$ == "e8" || .@w$ == "n8") setarray .@pts, 28951,50;
		if (.@w$ == "e16" || .@w$ == "n16") setarray .@pts, 28941,60;
		if (tt_point < .@pts[0]) set tt_point,tt_point+.@pts[1];
		warp strnpcinfo(4),384,161;
		enablenpc "Winner Helper#TBT_"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end2";
	}
	disablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";
	end;
	
	function	GetNumber	{
		setarray .@w_n$[1], "n4","n8","n16","e4","e8","e16";
		for (set .@i, 1; .@i <= getarraysize(.@w_n$); set .@i, .@i + 1) {
			if (getarg(0) == .@w_n$[.@i]) 
				return .@i;
		}		
	}
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";
	end;
}

	function	RName	{
		.@string$ = getarg(0);
		.@s = ( getstrlen(.@string$) > 2 ? substr(.@string$,1,2) : charat(.@string$,getstrlen(.@string$)-1) );
		set .@rn$,((compare(strnpcinfo(4),"_e_"+.@s))?"Expert mode":"Normal mode")+" - "+.@s+" person";
		return .@rn$;
	}
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "Winner Helper#TBT_"+.@w$;
	end;
}

	set .@st_to_sec,((.@hour_start * 3600) + (.@min_start * 60) + (.@sec_start));

				switch(select("木塊橋與方塊丘陵:詛咒沙漠與閃光迷宮:單一蝸牛與隱形迷宮:蛇骰與小洞窟:不用了，謝謝。")) {
				case 1:
					mes "[競速場地嚮導]";

					mes "起點。";
					next;
					mes "[競速場地嚮導]";
					mes "方塊丘陵是一座迷宮，";
					mes "由成堆的";
					mes "箱子組成。您可以爬過";
					mes "部分箱子，但";
					mes "，無法通過其他箱子。您必須摸索並找出最佳路線。";
					next;
					break;
				case 2:
					mes "[競速場地嚮導]";
					mes "詛咒沙漠是";
					mes "一條充滿";
					mes "驚喜的短程賽道。如果您真的";

					mes "[競速場地嚮導]";
					mes "閃光迷宮";

					mes "使人目眩的陷阱，您最好要格外";

					next;
					break;
				case 3:
					mes "[競速場地嚮導]";

				case 5:

					mes "我知道了。";
					mes "那好吧，";
					mes "祝";
					mes "您在比賽中好運！";
					next;
					set .@exitloop,0;
				}

			mes "[競速場地嚮導]";
			mes "您想要";
			mes "想知道其他任何";

		case 5:

			mes "在阿爾德巴蘭的";
			mes "競速場地時光，謝謝您~";
			close;
		}
	}

OnInit:
	// This will only ever be run ONCE!
	// Unless you eradicate the variable.
	//
	// This OnInit initiallizes and sets the starting
	// data for the Turbo Track's "Record" Logs.
	// WARNING/ATTENTION: DO NOT MODIFY ANY OF THE DATA.
	// DOING SO WILL CAUSE INTERNAL PROBLEMS IN THE SCRIPT.
	if ($ttnames$[0] == "") {
		setarray $ttnames$[0],"Breezy Havana";	// Solo Mode/Time Attack
		setarray $ttnames$[1],"RS125";	// Normal 4 Player
		setarray $ttnames$[2],"Hollgrehenn";	// Normal 8 Players
		setarray $ttnames$[3],"Antonio";	// Noomal 16 Players

		setarray $ttnames$[4],"Aragham";	// Expert 4 Players
		setarray $ttnames$[5],"Kafra Jasmine";	// Expert 8 Players
		setarray $ttnames$[6],"Chris";	// Expert 16 Players

		setarray $ttnames$[7],"Breezy Havana";	// Honor Place 1
		setarray $ttnames$[8],"RS125";	// Honor Place 2

		setarray $ttnames$[9],"Breezy Havana";	// Top 1
		setarray $ttnames$[10],"Nari";	// Top 2
		setarray $ttnames$[11],"Senorita Sylvia";	// Top 3
		setarray $ttnames$[12],"Joo Jahk";	// Top 4
		setarray $ttnames$[13],"RS125";	// Top 5

		setarray $ttranks[0],999999,0,0,0,0,0;
	}
	end;

OnEnd_n1:
	mapannounce "turbo_room","[普通模式 - 單人] 的比賽即將結束。",bc_map,"0xadff2f";
	end;

OnEnd_n4:
	mapannounce "turbo_room","[普通模式 - 4人] 的比賽即將結束。",bc_map,"0xadff2f";
	end;

OnEnd_n8:
	mapannounce "turbo_room","[普通模式 - 8人] 的比賽即將結束。",bc_map,"0xadff2f";
	end;

OnEnd_n16:
	mapannounce "turbo_room","[普通模式 - 16人] 的比賽即將結束。",bc_map,"0xadff2f";
	end;

OnEnd_e4:
	mapannounce "turbo_room","[專家模式 - 4人] 的比賽即將結束。",bc_map,"0xadff2f";
	end;

	mapannounce "turbo_room","[專家模式 - 8人] 的比賽即將結束。",bc_map,"0xadff2f";
	end;

OnEnd_e16:
	mapannounce "turbo_room","[專家模式 - 16人] 的比賽即將結束。",bc_map,"0xadff2f";
	end;

	end;

	end;

	mapannounce "turbo_room","恭喜！ " + $ttnames$[3] + " 剛贏得了一場 [普通模式 - 16人] 的比賽！",bc_map,"0x70DBDB";
	end;

OnWin_e4:
	mapannounce "turbo_room","恭喜！ " + $ttnames$[4] + " 剛贏得了一場 [專家模式 - 4人] 的比賽！",bc_map,"0x70DBDB";
	end;

OnWin_e8:

	end;

turbo_room,93,117,5	script	點數兌換助手::Point Exchange Helper	125,{
	if ((MaxWeight-Weight) < 2000 || checkweight(1201,1) == 0) {
		mes "^3355FF等一下！";
		mes "您目前攜帶了";
		mes "太多道具。";
		mes "請先";
		mes "請將部分";
		mes "道具透過卡普拉服務存放。^000000";
		close;
	}
	if (tt_point < 10) {
		mes "[道具兌換助手]";
		mes "您好，";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ".";
		mes "您總共有";
		mes "" + tt_point + " 競速場點數。";
		next;
		mes "[道具兌換助手]";
		mes "若您擁有至少";
		mes "10點競速場點數，";
		mes "就能將這些";
		mes "點數兌換成道具。您";
		mes "想看看競速場";
		mes "點數的兌換清單嗎？";
		next;
		if (select("是:取消") == 1) {
			mes "[道具兌換助手]";
			mes "^3355FF10 TTP^000000: 1張";
			mes "卡普拉傳送點 免費利用券";
			mes "^3355FF12 TTP^000000: 1張5級魔法卷軸";
			mes "^3355FF40 TTP^000000: 經驗值";
			next;
			mes "[道具兌換助手]";
			mes "^3355FF150 TTP^000000: 1顆3克拉鑽石";
			mes "^3355FF300 TTP^000000: 1個禮物箱";
			mes "^3355FF400 TTP^000000: 1瓶移速變化藥水";
			next;
			mes "[道具兌換助手]";
			mes "你也可以將競速場點數兌換成競技點數。如需更多資訊，請洽競技點數管理員。謝謝。";
			close;
		}
		mes "[道具兌換助手]";
		mes "希望您喜歡";
		mes "阿爾德巴蘭的競速場地。";
		mes "請盡量累積點數，";
		mes "以便之後能";
		mes "兌換有用的";
		mes "東西。祝比賽愉快~";
		close;
	}
	else {
		mes "[道具兌換助手]";
		mes "您好，";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ".";
		mes "您總共有";
		mes "" + tt_point + " 競速場點數。";
		mes "您要將";
		mes "這些點數兌換成道具嗎？";
		next;
		switch(select("兌換:取消")) {
		case 1:
			mes "[道具兌換助手]";
			mes "請選擇想要";
			mes "用來兌換";
			mes "您的競速場點數的道具。";
			next;
			switch(select("卡普拉傳送點 免費利用券:5級魔法卷軸:經驗值:3克拉鑽石:禮物箱:移速變化藥水:取消")) {
			case 1:
				mes "[道具兌換助手]";
				mes "每張免費利用券";
				mes "卡普拉傳送點";
				mes "需要10點競速場點數。";
				mes "請輸入您想要";
				mes "的數量。若要取消，";
				mes "請輸入「^3355FF0^000000」。";
				next;
				input .@input;
				if (.@input == 0) {
					mes "[道具兌換助手]";
					mes "您有";
					mes "已取消";
					mes "若您的請求";
					close;
				}
				else if (.@input < 0 || .@input > 50) {
					mes "[道具兌換助手]";
					mes "您的要求超過";
					mes "已超過上限。您";
					mes "一次最多只能領取";
					mes "50張免費利用券。";
					close;
				}
				else {

					if (.@total_point > tt_point) {
						mes "[道具兌換助手]";
						mes "很抱歉，您";
						mes "抱歉，您的競速場";
						mes "點數不足。請先確認";
						mes "您已獲得的競速場";
						mes "點數數量，";
						mes "再進行兌換。";
						close;
					}
					set tt_point,tt_point-.@total_point;
					getitem 7060,.@input; //Warp_Free_Ticket
					mes "[道具兌換助手]";
					mes "感謝您";
					mes "您的惠顧。";
					mes "希望您享受";

				}
			case 2:

				next;
				switch(select("地刺:冰箭:火焰箭:雷箭:靈魂攻擊:火球術:火牆:冰凍爆裂:治癒術")) {

				case 3:
					set .@scroll,691; //Fire_Scroll_1_5
					set .@name$,"Fire Bolt";
					break;
				case 4:
					set .@scroll,693; //Wind_Scroll_1_5
					set .@name$,"Lightning Bolt";
					break;
				case 5:
					set .@scroll,695; //Ghost_Scroll_1_5
					set .@name$,"Soul Strike";
					break;
				case 6:
					set .@scroll,697; //Fire_Scroll_2_5
					set .@name$,"Fire Ball";
					break;
				case 7:
					set .@scroll,699; //Fire_Scroll_3_5
					set .@name$,"Fire Wall";
					break;
				case 8:
					set .@scroll,12000; //Cold_Scroll_2_5
					set .@name$,"Frost Diver";
					break;
				case 9:
					set .@scroll,12002; //Holy_Scroll_1_5
					set .@name$,"Heal";
					break;
				}
				mes "[道具兌換助手]";
				mes "請輸入";
				mes "的 ^4d4dff"+.@name$+"^000000 張魔法卷軸，";
				mes "您想領取的數量。";
				mes "若要取消，請輸入''^3355FF0^000000''。";
				next;
				input .@input;
				if (.@input == 0) {
					mes "[道具兌換助手]";
					mes "您有";
					mes "已取消";
					mes "若您的請求";
					close;
				}
				else if (.@input < 0 || .@input > 50) {
					mes "[道具兌換助手]";
					mes "很抱歉，你的";
					mes "您的請求已超過";
					mes "上限。您一次最多只能";
					mes "請求50張卷軸。";
					close;
				}
				else {
					set .@total_point,12 * .@input;
					if (.@total_point > tt_point) {
						mes "[道具兌換助手]";
						mes "很抱歉，您";
						mes "抱歉，您的競速場";
						mes "點數不足。請先確認";
						mes "您已獲得的競速場";
						mes "點數數量，";
						mes "再進行兌換。";
						close;
					}
					set tt_point,tt_point-.@total_point;
					getitem .@scroll,.@input;
					mes "[道具兌換助手]";
					mes "感謝您";
					mes "您的惠顧。";
					mes "希望您享受";
					mes "在此";
					mes "競速場地的時光~";
					close;
				}
			case 3:
				mes "[道具兌換助手]";

turbo_room,106,117,3	script	點數管理員#tt::Point Manager#tt	833,{
	mes "[競速場點數管理員]";

	mes "競速場的時光愉快嗎？";

	next;
	switch(select("查詢點數:兌換點數:^660000兌換說明^000000")) {
	case 1:
		mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ",";
		mes "您目前擁有";
		mes "" + tt_point + " 競速場點數";
		mes "和 " + arena_point + " 點競技點數。";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ",";
		mes "您目前擁有";
		mes "" + tt_point + " 競速場點數";

		next;

		mes "只能以競技點數";
		mes "^4D4DFF10的倍數^000000進行兌換。";
		next;
		switch(select("2 AP -> 1 TTP:4 AP -> 2 TTP:6 AP -> 3 TTP:8 AP -> 4 TTP:10 AP以上:取消")) {
		case 1:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28999,2,1;
		case 2:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28998,4,2;
		case 3:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28997,6,2;
		case 4:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28996,8,4;
		case 5:
			mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
			mes "請輸入您想將10點競技點數兌換為競速場點數的次數。可輸入的最大值為";
			mes "20。若要取消，請輸入「^3355FF0^000000」。";
			next;
			input .@input;
			if (.@input <= 0) {
				mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
				mes "您有";
				mes "已取消";
				mes "若您的請求";
				close;
			}
			else if (.@input > 20) {
				mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
				mes "您的要求超過";
				mes "上限。請";
				mes "輸入不大於20的數值。";
				close;
			}
			else {
				set .@want_point1,10 * .@input;
				set .@want_point,5 * .@input;
				set .@my_turbo_all,tt_point+.@want_point;
				set .@my_arena_all,arena_point-.@want_point1;
				if (.@my_turbo_all > 28999) {
					mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
					mes "很抱歉，若繼續兌換，您的競速場點數將超過上限。請先花費更多";
					mes "競速場點數後再使用本服務。謝謝。";
					close;
				}
				if (.@my_arena_all < 0) {
					mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
					mes "很抱歉，您";
					mes "沒有足夠的競技點數";
					mes "無法進行本次競速場";
					mes "點數兌換。";
					close;
				}
				else {
					mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
					mes "你已兌換";
					mes "將10點競技點數兌換為";
					mes "競速場點數 " + .@input + " 次。";
					mes "總計 " + .@want_point1 + " 競技點數";
					mes "已兌換為";
					mes "" + .@want_point +" 競速場點數。";
					set arena_point,.@my_arena_all; //arena_point
					set tt_point,.@my_turbo_all;
					next;
					mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
					mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ",";
					mes "你現在擁有";
					mes "^00688B" + tt_point + "^000000 競速場點數";
					mes "並 ^4682B4" + arena_point + "^000000 競技點數。";
					mes "感謝您的惠顧。";
					close;
				}
			}
		case 6:
			mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
			mes "您有";
			mes "已取消";
			mes "若您的請求";
			close;
		}
	case 3:
		// There is no dialog for this option.
		// It's possible it's missing, or maybe
		// the dialog was never translated. XD
		//??
		close;
	}

S_ExchangePoints:
	if (tt_point > getarg(0)) {
		mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
		mes "很抱歉，若繼續兌換，您的競速場點數將超過上限。請先花費更多";
		mes "競速場點數後再使用本服務。謝謝。";
		close;
	}
	if (arena_point >= getarg(1)) {
		mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
		mes "依您的要求，";
		mes "2點競技點數";
		mes "已兌換";
		mes "為1點競速場點數。";
		set arena_point,arena_point-getarg(1);
		set tt_point,tt_point+getarg(2);
		next;
		mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ",";
		mes "你現在擁有";
		mes "^00688B" + tt_point + "^000000 競速場點數";
		mes "並 ^4682B4" + arena_point + "^000000 競技點數。";
		mes "感謝您的惠顧。";
		close;
	}
	else {
		mes "[競速場點數管理員]";
		mes "很抱歉，您沒有足夠的競技點數。您需要";
		mes "至少2點競技點數才能";
		mes "使用本服務。";
		close;
	}
}

			warp "alde_gld",183,199;
			end;

			setoption Option_Wugrider,0;
			warp "turbo_room",100,65;
		}
	} else if ((Class == Job_Mechanic || Class == Job_Mechanic_T || Class == Job_Baby_Mechanic) && checkmadogear()) {

			mes "[助手]";

	end;
}

-	script	::MountManager_turbo	-1,{
	set .@n$, "["+strnpcinfo(1)+"]";
	mes .@n$;
	if (ismounting()) {
		mes "請從您騎乘的生物上下來。";
		mes "然後再來找我。";
		close;
	}
	if (BaseJob == Job_Knight || BaseJob == Job_Crusader) {
		if (!(eaclass()&EAJL_THIRD)) {
			if (BaseJob == Job_Knight) {
				set .@zeny,2500;
				set .@zeny$,"2,500";
				set .@mount$,"Peco Peco";
			} else {
				set .@zeny,3500;
				set .@zeny$,"3,500";
				set .@mount$,"Grand Peco";
			}
			set .@skill,63;
			set .@skill$,"Peco Peco Ride";
			set .@riding, checkriding();
			set .@i,1;
		} else {
			if (BaseJob == Job_Knight) {
				set .@mount$,"Dragon";
				set .@skill,63;
				set .@skill$,"Dragon Training";
				set .@riding, checkdragon();
				set .@i,2;
			} else {
				set .@mount$,"Gryphon";
				set .@skill,63;
				set .@skill$,"Peco Peco Ride";
				set .@riding, checkriding();
				set .@i,1;
			}
		}
	} else if (Class == Job_Mechanic || Class == Job_Mechanic_T || Class == Job_Baby_Mechanic) {
		set .@mount$,"Magic Gear";
		set .@skill,2255;
		set .@skill$,"Magic Gear License";
		set .@riding, checkmadogear();
		set .@i,3;
	} else {
		mes "感謝您";
		mes "來到阿爾德巴蘭的";
		mes "競速場地~";
		close;
	}
	mes "歡迎，您是否想";
	mes "取回您的 "+.@mount$+"?";
	if (.@zeny) {
		mes "請出示您的免費";
		mes "大嘴鳥使用券。您";
		mes "也可以支付租借費";
		mes "共 "+.@zeny$+" Zeny。";
	}
	next;
	switch(select("是:取消")) {
	case 1:
		if (getskilllv(.@skill) == 0) {
			mes .@n$;
			mes "很抱歉，你";
			mes "沒有資格使用";
			mes "這項服務。請先去學";
			mes "需先學會 "+.@skill$+" 技能。";
			close;
		} else {
			if (.@riding) {
				mes .@n$;
				mes "你已經";
				mes "騎著";
				mes .@mount$+".";
				mes "謝謝你~";
				close;
			} else {
				if (.@zeny) {
					if (countitem(7310) < 1 && Zeny < .@zeny) {
						mes .@n$;
						mes "很抱歉，您";
						mes "您沒有免費使用券";
						mes "大嘴鳥免費使用券，或 "+.@zeny$+" Zeny。";
						mes "使用大嘴鳥租借服務。";
						close;
					} else {
						if (countitem(7310) > 0)
							delitem 7310,1; //Free_Peco_Ticket
						else
							set Zeny, Zeny-.@zeny;
					}
				}
				switch(.@i) {
					case 1: setriding; break;
					case 2: setdragon; break;
					case 3: setmadogear; break;
				}
				mes .@n$;
				mes "感謝您";
				mes "您的惠顧~";
				close;
			}
		}
	case 2:
		mes .@n$;
		mes "您是否要";
		mes "返回";
		mes "競速場地比賽？";
		mes "祝你好運！";
		close;
	}
}
